#### [Create a poppy suncatcher](https://www.trumba.com/calendars/brisbane-city-council?eventid=205243101)
📆 Tue 29 Sep – Wed 30 Sep
📍 Anzac Square, Brisbane City, Anzac Square, 228 Adelaide Street, Brisbane City
💰 Free
`art` `craft` `history` `kids` `family` `free`

Pop into Anzac Square Memorial Galleries during the spring school holidays to create your own poppy suncatcher. Let's get crafty! Suitable for all ages, this free family-friendly craft activity is available between 10 am – 4pm daily from 20 September to 4 October (closed Saturdays). Bookings are not required, simply visit the craft table in our Post World War II Gallery. Facilitated by our knowledgeable team, this activity will explore the special connection between poppies and Australia’s military past. A resilient flower, the red poppy was amongst the first to return to bloom in the fields of France and Belgium after World War I, emerging as a symbol of remembrance for the Allied soldiers who fell in battle. Once you've created your poppy suncatcher, stay to explore the Memorial Galleries, embarking on an interactive journey from World War I to today, before taking time to reflect on the remarkable stories of bravery and sacrifice in the tranquil parklands.

#### [Clubbercise fitness class](https://tidycal.com/breekwilliams/clubbercisebrackenridge)
📆 Tue 29 Sep, 6:30 PM
📍 Bracken Ridge Hall, Bracken Ridge, Bracken Ridge Hall, 77 Bracken Street, Bracken Ridge
💰 See description
`fitness` `dance` `music`

Ready to party your way fit? Clubbercise is a high-energy, easy-to-follow dance workout set to your favourite club anthems from the 90s to now - think disco lights, fun vibes, and serious feel-good energy. Join us getting our party on with flashing lights, pumping beats, and a room full of legends just like you - ready to move, glow, and feel amazing. You work at your own level, we keep it simple, and together we enjoy the music! Wear your most comfortable clothes (we encourage a bit of fluoro flair), lace up your trainers, and bring a towel and water bottle. You’ll leave feeling energised, empowered, and totally alive. Because you don’t just want to work out… you want to have a blast doing it - right? Class prices: $30 casual $55 for 2 classes $125 for 5 classes (glowsticks to keep) $240 for 10 classes (glowsticks to keep). Requirements: Wear comfy shoes and clothes (we love a bit of fluoro!). Bring a towel & water bottle. If it's your first class, please come in 10mins early to fill in a health questionnaire. Walk ins are welcome. Cash or Bank Transfer accepted. No dance experience needed - just come to move, glow, and feel good. Classes are weekly!

Facilitated by our knowledgeable team, this activity will explore the special connection between poppies and Australia’s military past. #### [School holiday activity - Spring hub - Flower power](https://www.eventbrite.com.au/e/school-holiday-activity-spring-hub-flower-power-tickets-1997534094248)
📆 Thu 1 Oct, 10:00 AM
📍 Brisbane Botanic Gardens Mt Coot-tha, Brisbane Botanic Gardens Mt Coot-tha, 152 Mt Coot-tha Road, Toowong
💰 Free
`science` `nature` `art` `kids` `family` `free`

Discover the fascinating world of flowers and the peculiar Pollinators who work with them at our Spring hub school holiday activities at the Brisbane Botanic Gardens Mt Coot-tha. Take off on a scientific exploration of our Sensory Garden, bursting with blooms of all shapes and sizes and bring your findings back to the hub where you can take a closer look with microscopes and magnifying glasses. Get your hands dirty and turn science into art by making 3D clay pollen models and sensational seed balls. Finally compile all your field notes and decorate them with pen or a paintbrush while you discover more about the precious pollinators who help to keep our flowers fantastic and our fruit plentiful. Bring a picnic blanket and lunch to enjoy on our grassy lawn. Note: Children under 16 are to be accompanied by an adult. Location : Meet at the Auditorium lawn. Please wear a hat and enclosed shoes; and bring a keep cup or water bottle. This workshop supports the Botanic Gardens' commitment to creating a plant loving community dedicated to putting the plant back into planet. For more information, send an email .

#### [Create a poppy suncatcher](https://www.trumba.com/calendars/brisbane-city-council?eventid=205243097)
📆 Fri 25 Sep – Sat 26 Sep
📍 Anzac Square, Brisbane City, Anzac Square, 228 Adelaide Street, Brisbane City
💰 See link
`art` `craft` `history` `kids` `family`

Pop into Anzac Square Memorial Galleries during the spring school holidays to create your own poppy suncatcher. Let's get crafty! Suitable for all ages, this free family-friendly craft activity is available between 10am–4pm daily from 20 September to 4 October (closed Saturdays). Bookings are not required, simply visit the craft table in our Post World War II Gallery. Facilitated by our knowledgeable team, this activity will explore the special connection between poppies and Australia’s military past. A resilient flower, the red poppy was amongst the first to return to bloom in the fields of France and Belgium after World War I, emerging as a symbol of remembrance for the Allied soldiers who fell in battle. Once you've created your poppy suncatcher, stay to explore the Memorial Galleries, embarking on an interactive journey from World War I to today, before taking time to reflect on the remarkable stories of bravery and sacrifice in the tranquil parklands.

💰 $5.00 AUD

Come and sit around the (pretend!) campfire with author and speech pathologist Cerise Madsen and discover what your own unique Aussie animal force is these school holidays! Join us for a magical hour inspired by Animal Force, a wildlife adventure packed with fun, danger and epic animal powers. Cerise will read a spellbinding excerpt from the book, then it’s over to you. Meet the animal heroes and characters from the story, learn about the real life superpowers of our Australian animals, choose your own animal force and bring it to life with a colourful craft activity. Along the way we’ll play fun camping word games, learn some fun key word signs for Aussie animals, and discover our own inner light. Perfect for curious kids 7+ who love stories, animals and a little bit of magic. Bookings required. This event is $5/child, redeemable off any purchase of Animal Force before or on the day. Parents are welcome to drop off their kids and go-- staff members are blue card-certified. Please note: the booking confirmation is an e-ticket. Please be sure to check your spam/junk folder.

Adapted from Colleen Hoover's best-selling novel, this seductive, psychological thriller follows renowned author Verity Crawford (Anne Hathaway) and Lowen Ashleigh (Dakota Johnson), a struggling writer who relocates to the remote Crawford estate to ghostwrite for Verity. After Lowen uncovers what appears to be Verity's chilling autobiographical notes, she wrestles with the disturbing and twisted confessions about Verity’s husband Jeremy (Josh Hartnett), and finds it hard to separate fiction from reality, manipulation from attraction, and opportunity from obsession.

📆 Sat 3 Oct, 9:30 AM
📍 Karawatha Forest Park and Discovery Centre, Karawatha Forest Park and Discovery Centre, 149 Acacia Road, Karawatha
💰 Free
`community` `nature` `fitness` `walking` `outdoor` `free`

Our new community walking group is a relaxed, social way to explore Brisbane’s beautiful parks and green spaces. Walking in a group is a safe and supportive way to explore bushland, especially for those who may be unfamiliar with the area or prefer not to walk alone. Along the way, you might pick up interesting facts about local flora, fauna, and history, while enjoying the many benefits of walking, from improved health, community connection, and time in nature. Requirements: Safety first! Please wear closed-in walking shoes, bring a water bottle, and dress in sun-protective clothing including a hat and sunscreen. Walks may include uneven terrain, uphill sections of the walk. Note: This walk may be cancelled if unsafe walking conditions arise on the day, including severe weather, extreme heat, or any other hazards that may impact participant safety.

`theatre` `music` `dance` `circus` `performance` `festival`

This year, the Festival moves with the rhythm of Brisbane itself. Inspired by Brisbane being the world’s earliest rising city, the program stretches from First Light through to After Dark. Across every corner of the program — from theatre, music, dance, circus, and puppetry to intimate encounters and large-scale spectacles — you will discover World Premieres, Australian Exclusives, and extraordinary artists from Queensland, across Australia, and around the globe. Discover the full 2026 festival program now via the Brisbane Festival website. Brisbane Festival is an initiative of the Queensland Government and Brisbane City Council.

#### [Verso Projects. Festival Village, South Bank](https://www.brisbanefestival.com.au/events)
📆 Fri 4 Sep – Sun 27 Sep
📍 South Bank, Brisbane City, South Bank, corner Ernest Street, Stanley Street and Grey Street, South Brisbane
💰 Free
`live music` `music` `dj` `nightlife` `performance` `art`

The civic heart of Brisbane Festival. From sunrise to last call Verso Projects. Festival Village, South Bank is the civic heart of Brisbane Festival – a riverside gathering place alive with music, performance, food, art and nightlife from sunrise to late night. As the city wakes, mornings begin with sunrise rituals, soundscapes, movement and daybreak dance parties by the river. Through the day, the Village hums with free activities for all ages – giant singalongs, pop-up performances, workshops, conversations and moments of surprise around every corner. Then, as night falls, the energy shifts. Bright Nights by ANZ transforms the skyline in an explosion of sound, water and light, and the Village switches on after dark with live music, DJs and late-night revelry. Come for a headline show or stumble into something completely unexpected. Grab a drink, pull up a seat by the river, follow the lights, join the party and stay for one more. The Village is where the city gathers to switch on, light up and come alive together. Brisbane Festival is an initiative of the Queensland Government and Brisbane City Council. Discover the full 2026 festival program now via the Brisbane Festival website.

#### [South Brisbane Cemetery moonlight tour](https://fosbc.au/tours/moonlight-tour/)
📆 Fri 25 Sep, 7:30 PM
📍 South Brisbane Cemetery, Dutton Park, South Brisbane Cemetery, 185 Annerley Road, Dutton Park
💰 From $10
`tour` `history` `storytelling`

Where it all began – a cemetery, a torchlight, and a thousand untold stories. Step back into the roots of Brisbane’s most beloved cemetery experiences on a Moonlight Tour , the original night-time walk that started it all. First held back circa 2009, this was the tour that lit the path – quite literally – for every Friends of South Brisbane Cemetery tour that’s followed. Unlike other tours with fixed scripts and set stops, the Moonlight Tour is delightfully unpredictable. It flows with the mood of the night and the curiosity of the group. No two nights are the same, because no two walks through history should ever be! As you meander beneath the stars and the towering trees of South Brisbane Cemetery, your guides – Tracey Olivieri and Liam Baker – will share the real stories that have shaped this extraordinary place. They’ve spent years delving into the archives, decades amongst the headstones, and countless evenings sharing their passion for the cemetery with others. You might hear about: The floods that swallowed the past The evolution of headstone art and forgotten funerary symbols The graves of the executed, tucked quietly into a shaded corner How faith divided the dead in life, and in burial The controversial “beautification” that reshaped an historical landscape A whispered ghost story (or two)…for those who like their history with a little chill And whatever questions you bring - because on this tour, you help steer the story! This is history as it should be: lived, shared, and responsive to the wonder of the moment. Whether you’re returning to where it all began or joining us for the very first time, the Moonlight Tour offers a rare and immersive way to explore South Brisbane Cemetery – not just as a resting place, but as a living archive of lives once lived. Bring your torch. Bring your curiosity. The story is still unfolding. All of our tours are not-for-profit , with all money raised going to our cemetery history and maintenance projects.

#### [Convict Brisbane Walking Tour](https://museumofbrisbane.t.qtix.com.au/events/bc8f0b59-203b-7684-a252-b2a747797dda?_sm_vck=nvj704HfPStDF3D2f54HmHZq536B0vNrqttsMRM7NT7047TWMSqs)
📆 Sat 26 Sep, 10:30 AM
📍 Museum of Brisbane, Brisbane City, Museum of Brisbane, Brisbane City Hall, 64 Adelaide Street, Brisbane City
💰 From $30
`history` `tour` `walking` `indigenous` `outdoor`

Explore Brisbane’s colonial history on this guided tour through the city’s convict past. Beginning at the historic Windmill on Wickham Terrace and winding down to the river at Queens Wharf, the tour traces Brisbane’s transformation between 1823 and 1842 - from a remote penal outpost to an emerging free town. Hear stories of convicts and commandants, revealing how punishment, labour and authority shaped daily life. Along the way, our guides bring the settlement into focus, illuminating the social fabric and living conditions, as well as the central role of the Brisbane River in sustaining the colony. Woven throughout the tour are First Peoples’ responses to British settlement, their enduring connection to Country, and the Frontier wars that directly followed this era. Together, these intersecting stories offer a deeper understanding of how Brisbane grew from its earliest foundations into a regional centre. Tour highlights include: The Windmill, The Brisbane Crest, The Petrie Tableau, Convict-built structures and surviving remnants, Miller Park and the Commissariat Stores, Queens Wharf and, the Brisbane River. This 90‑minute, 1.75km tour is designed to suit most fitness levels, however, please note it includes walking downhill along city streets and across uneven surfaces. For more information about accessibility requirements, please refer to the Museum of Brisbane FAQs . Museum of Brisbane is Brisbane City Council’s leading history and art museum where you can experience our city’s vibrant culture.

#### [Celebrate the Moon Festival in the Valley](https://myvalley.com.au/moon-festival)

One of the most auspicious dates on the Asian calendars, the Moon Festival (or Mid-Autumn Festival), occurs on the date when the moon is at its fullest. Gather your friends and family for one of the most vibrant cultural events of the year. On Saturday 26 September, Fortitude Valley will come alive with colour, music and tradition as the Moon Festival returns. From spectacular lion and dragon dances to lantern making workshops and interactive performances, the Valley is the place to be for an evening of free entertainment, food and festivity. Savour authentic Asian dining at your favourite Valley restaurant and soak up the community spirit that makes this celebration unforgettable. Visit the My Valley website for more information.

#### [Volunteer Bushcare Working Bee - McDowall Bushcare Group](http://leswat50@gmail.com/)
📆 Tue 29 Sep, 8:30 AM
📍 McDowall Reserve, McDowall Reserve, Corner of Speilberg Street and Stallone Circuit, McDowall
💰 Free
`community` `environment` `nature` `free`

Join other like-minded volunteers to help protect and restore Brisbane’s unique biodiversity through our Habitat Brisbane program. Whatever your skill level and motivation, joining your local Habitat Brisbane group is a great way to connect to nature and be active and healthy, all while meeting new people, developing new skills and making a difference to your local environment. With 160 groups across the city there’s bound to be one near you! Habitat Brisbane volunteers undertake a variety of activities including: managing environmental weeds revegetating with local native plants creating habitat for native wildlife citizen science projects. The McDowall Bushcare Group meet every Tuesday from 8.30-11.30am.

#### [Crosby Road bushcare](https://www.trumba.com/calendars/brisbane-city-council?eventid=206430283)
📆 Sat 3 Oct, 7:00 AM
📍 Crosby Road Park, Ascot, Crosby Road Park, 150 Crosby Road, Ascot
💰 Free
`environment` `nature` `gardening` `community` `free`

Help regenerate a precious patch of urban bush below Bartley’s Hill Lookout in Ascot. We plant native species to attract birds, bees, bats and lizards, while removing the invasive plants that crowd them. Join any monthly working bee starting at 7am on the first Saturday of every month except January. Requirements: Wear protective clothing, a hat and gloves. Insect repellent recommended in summer. No experience necessary. Meeting point: We meet near bus stop 21 Bushview on the north side of Crosby Road - look for the container with a bat and butterfly mural.

#### [Guided walk of the Billai Dhagun Circuit](https://www.trumba.com/calendars/brisbane-city-council?eventid=206354441)
📆 Sat 3 Oct, 9:30 AM
📍 Boondall Wetlands Environment Centre, Boondall Wetlands Environment Centre, 31 Paperbark Drive (entry via Stanworth Rd), Boondall
💰 Free
`nature` `wildlife` `tour` `outdoor` `free`

Join experienced Volunteer Guides in a guided walk through the stunning Boondall Wetlands Reserve. Learn about the unique plants and animals of the wetlands on this gentle, easy, all abilities 2km walk. Participants can visit the Bird Hide, observe distinct changes in vegetation, and discover why Boondall Wetlands is an internationally important conservation area. Requirements: all children must be accompanied by an adult wear a wide-brimmed hat, secure and fully enclosed walking shoes, sun-smart clothing, sunscreen, and insect repellent bring drinking water and a snack or picnic lunch as there are no food outlets on site arrive 10minutes early. Note: Walks are subject to weather and environmental conditions.

} } ⚖️ Watch drama | 🗳️ Cast a vote | 🕵️ Crack the case ⭐ You've been called for jury duty. Step into a thrilling courtroom drama where the audience is the jury. Listen to testimonies, analyze evidence, and ultimately decide… guilty or not guilty? It's not theatre you watch. It's justice you deliver. The Million Dollar Murder Inspired by true events, this gripping courtroom drama pulls you into a world where greed, passion, and revenge collide — and where the line between victim and villain is razor-thin. The accused stands in the dock, but is this a cold-blooded killer, or a pawn in a far more dangerous game? No DNA evidence. A tangled web of desire and betrayal. And a divorce worth millions. Every witness has a reason to lie. Every motive points somewhere different. Can you untangle the truth before the verdict is cast? 🚩 This experience includes themes of murder and violent death, domestic violence, emotional manipulation. There is a detailed discussion of a homicide. The performance contains moments of intense emotional conflict. Highlights 🤩 Enjoy a live theatre courtroom drama where you decide the final verdict 🫵 Vote during key moments - your decisions shape hidden angles in the case, making every show different! 🕵️‍♂️ Dissect witness testimonies, unpack evidence, and weigh every argument 🎢 A high-stakes case full of twists - more turns than a rollercoaster, and a verdict only you can deliver How it works 1 Take your seat Arrive 30 min early. Doors close at show time — late entry is not permitted. 2 Hear the case Track every testimony, spot the contradictions, and vote at key moments to steer the trial. 3 Deliver your verdict The jury speaks. Guilty or not guilty - the outcome rests entirely with you. Practical info ⏳ Duration 60 minutes Doors open 30 min before 📍 Venue Old Museum Brisbane 480 Gregory Terrace, Bowen Hills, QLD 4006, Australia 👤 Age 12+ Under 16s must be with an adult ♿ Accessibility Wheelchair accessible Wheelchair zone available ✨ Groups & Private Take the stand 30+ people or private show 🎁 Gift card Send gift Gift memories to someone special Frequently asked questions About the experience What is The Jury Experience and what can I expect? The Jury Experience is an immersive courtroom drama where you become part of the story as a juror. Analyze testimonies, cross-examinations, and evidence to uncover the truth - your vote determines the accused's fate. How long does the experience last? The experience lasts approximately 1 hour. Will there be strobe lights? No, there will not be any strobe lights. Are seats numbered? No, seats are not assigned. Seating is first-come, first-served within the zone you select when purchasing your ticket. About your tickets What are the different types of tickets available? A reserve: Best visibility B reserve: Good visibility, located on the sides C reserve: Fair visibility, positioned further back D reserve: Fair visibility, positioned further back behind C reserve Can I buy a ticket on site on the day of the event? Tickets cannot be purchased on site. All tickets must be purchased via the app or website (feverup.com) before arriving at the event. Is it necessary to buy a ticket for my carer? Guests requiring assistance are entitled to one free pass for their personal assistant/support worker. Contact us to receive a voucher for a free carer ticket (based on availability). The regular ticket must be shown together with the carer ticket at entry. Can I change the date of my ticket? Are refunds allowed? Subject to availability, date/time changes are permitted up to 48 hours before the start time. Tickets are non-refundable except as required under Australian Consumer Law. Can I transfer my tickets to another person? Yes, tickets can be transferred to any other Fever account at no additional cost via "Transfer tickets" in the Fever app. Where can I find my ticket? Do I need to print it out? Your ticket is in the "Tickets" section of the Fever app, and also in your purchase confirmation email. You do not need to print it - it's validated via the app on arrival. Access & venue Where does the show take place? The show takes place at The Old Museum, 480 Gregory Terrace, Bowen Hills QLD 4006, Australia. Does the experience take place indoors or outdoors? This experience takes place indoors in a heated/air-conditioned venue. Are food and drinks available on site for purchase? No, food and drinks are not available for purchase on site. Are animals allowed? Animals, except assistance animals, are not allowed. Is the venue accessible? Yes, the event is held in an accessible venue. Accessible seating is limited to Zone A and B only. Are there chairs available? Can I be seated during the experience? Yes, this is a seated experience - seating is available for every guest. Will toilet facilities be available on site? Yes, the venue has toilets; on-site staff can direct you. Is there parking on site? There is no dedicated venue parking, but surrounding parking garages and limited street parking are available. Public transport or sustainable alternatives are recommended. Is cloakroom service available? Unfortunately, this location does not have a cloakroom service. Can I take photos or use my phone during the performance? Photos and videos can only be taken during the curtain call, without flash. Phones must be in silent mode throughout the performance. Can I leave during the performance? What items are prohibited? Out of respect for the performers and the audience, you may not leave your seat except in an emergency. Prohibited items include smoking/vaping, external alcohol, hot food or drinks, strong-smelling food or glass, audio recording devices, drugs, flammables, torches, lasers, professional cameras, promotional items, and selfie sticks/tripods. Seating Map Murder #1 · The Million Dollar Murder

Sherwood Arboretum, established in 1925, is Brisbane’s only botanic garden dedicated exclusively to growing Australian native trees. The Arboretum holds scientific and cultural values with many of the original plantings undertaken by prominent citizens of Brisbane. The guided walk will be led by the Volunteer Guides of the Brisbane Botanic Gardens Mt Coot-tha and the Friends of Sherwood Arboretum. Requirements: Wear a hat, enclosed walking shoes, and be sure to bring a water bottle and sunscreen. Meeting point: Meet at the shelter adjacent to the main carpark off Jolimont Street, Sherwood. Age: Open to all ages. Children must be accompanied by an adult. Note : Walks may be subject to environmental conditions and guide capacity on the day.

#### [Meet Wildlife Neighbours (6+ years)](https://www.eventbrite.com.au/e/meet-wildlife-neighbours-6-years-tickets-1997946523836?aff=oddtdtcreator)
📆 Wed 30 Sep, 10:30 AM
📍 Karawatha Forest Park and Discovery Centre, Karawatha Forest Park and Discovery Centre, 149 Acacia Road, Karawatha
💰 Free
`nature` `wildlife` `science` `kids` `free`

Get up close and discover the feathered, furry and scaly friends that live in our neighbourhoods. Brisbane is Australia’s most biodiverse capital city. Native wildlife live all around us — from our backyards to local parks and nature reserves. But have you ever met these unique animals face to face? Join our wildlife expert for an engaging and immersive session in nature, where you'll meet your wild neighbours and some of Brisbane's most iconic animals. Discover what makes them special, how they live, and the vital role they play in our ecosystems. Your actions matter. By learning more about our native species, you’ll be empowered to make simple changes — like planting native vegetation or keeping pets indoors at night. These actions help protect our incredible biodiversity and ensure our wildlife neighbours continue to thrive. Requirements on the day: Arrive minimum 10 minutes early to check in with staff at the centre. All children must be accompanied and directly supervised by an adult. Wear a hat, sun-safe clothing, fully enclosed shoes, sunscreen, and bring insect repellent as some shows may take place outside. Bring water and a snack if required, as there are no food outlets on site. Note: Events are subject to weather and environmental conditions and may be cancelled on the day.

#### [Convict Brisbane Walking Tour](https://museumofbrisbane.t.qtix.com.au/events/bc8f0b59-203b-7684-a252-b2a747797dda?_sm_vck=nvj704HfPStDF3D2f54HmHZq536B0vNrqttsMRM7NT7047TWMSqs)
📆 Sat 3 Oct, 10:30 AM
📍 Museum of Brisbane, Brisbane City, Museum of Brisbane, Brisbane City Hall, 64 Adelaide Street, Brisbane City
💰 From $30
`history` `tour` `culture`

Explore Brisbane’s colonial history on this guided tour through the city’s convict past. Beginning at the historic Windmill on Wickham Terrace and winding down to the river at Queens Wharf, the tour traces Brisbane’s transformation between 1823 and 1842 - from a remote penal outpost to an emerging free town. Hear stories of convicts and commandants, revealing how punishment, labour and authority shaped daily life. Along the way, our guides bring the settlement into focus, illuminating the social fabric and living conditions, as well as the central role of the Brisbane River in sustaining the colony. Woven throughout the tour are First Peoples’ responses to British settlement, their enduring connection to Country, and the Frontier wars that directly followed this era. Together, these intersecting stories offer a deeper understanding of how Brisbane grew from its earliest foundations into a regional centre. Tour highlights include: The Windmill, The Brisbane Crest, The Petrie Tableau, Convict-built structures and surviving remnants, Miller Park and the Commissariat Stores, Queens Wharf and, the Brisbane River. This 90‑minute, 1.75km tour is designed to suit most fitness levels, however, please note it includes walking downhill along city streets and across uneven surfaces. For more information about accessibility requirements, please refer to the Museum of Brisbane FAQs . Museum of Brisbane is Brisbane City Council’s leading history and art museum where you can experience our city’s vibrant culture.

#### [Passport to the Universe](https://www.trumba.com/calendars/brisbane-city-council?eventid=208417953)

💰 Adult $17.50, Child (3-14 yrs) $11, Concession $13.50
`science`

Get your passport for a thrilling journey through the Solar System, out of the Milky Way Galaxy and to the edge of the observable universe, returning home via a black hole. Narrated by Tom Hanks. Session concludes with a virtual tour of the Brisbane night sky. Passport to the Universe is suitable for ages 8 and over. For younger children, we recommend Perfect Little Planet or Tycho Goes to Mars . #### [Clock Tower Tour](https://www.eventbrite.com.au/e/clock-tower-tours-tickets-869613355957)
📆 Wed 30 Sep, 10:15 AM
📍 Museum of Brisbane, Brisbane City, Museum of Brisbane, Brisbane City Hall, 64 Adelaide Street, Brisbane City
💰 Free
`history` `tour` `free`

Discover the hidden secrets of your heritage-listed City Hall building with a guided tour of Brisbane’s iconic Clock Tower. The Clock Tower Tour is a “must do” while in Brisbane! For many years, City Hall’s Clock Tower elevated the building to Brisbane’s tallest, offering visitors a magnificent 360 degree view of the city around them. Whilst the view has changed significantly over the last 90 years, the time-honoured tradition of “taking a trip up the tower” happily continues at Museum of Brisbane. The Clock Tower Tour includes a ride in one of Brisbane’s oldest working cage lifts, a look behind Australia’s largest analogue clock faces and time to explore the observation platform that shares a unique perspective of your city. See if you can catch a glimpse of the bells! Make a booking online today to secure your spot! Spaces are limited and fill up quickly. Date & Time 10:15am - 4:45pm Daily, every 15 minutes Please arrive 15 minutes before your tour.

#### [German National Day](https://www.brisbanegermanweek.com.au/)
📆 Sat 3 Oct – Sun 4 Oct
📍 Brisbane City Council Creative Events
💰 Free
`culture` `history` `free`

German National Day, marks the reunification of Germany in 1990 after more than four decades of division. It commemorates the peaceful path to unity that followed the fall of the Berlin Wall and remains Germany’s most important national celebration. In Brisbane, iconic landmarks will shine in the German colours black red and gold to mark the occasion. Please note: This event is part of Brisbane German Week. For more information, please visit Brisbane German Week Website . This event is proudly supported by Brisbane City Council.

#### [Tejaswini Bhava Abhiyan](https://www.trumba.com/calendars/brisbane-city-council?eventid=209198357)
📆 Sun 4 Oct, 5:00 PM
📍 Northgate Hall, Northgate, Northgate Hall, 34 Ridge Street, Northgate
💰 Free
`community` `culture` `women` `youth` `free`

Tejaswini Bhava Abhiyan — A girls and women only event. Tejaswini Abhiyaan is a community initiative organised by Shri Yog Vedant Seva Samiti, in association with the Mahila Utthan Mandal wing of Sant Shri Asharam Ji Ashram, with the aim of empowering girls and young women by nurturing confidence, positive thinking, inner strength, self-belief, and a strong sense of purpose. The word Tejaswini describes a girl who embodies tejas (inner radiance), confidence, positivity, courage, self-esteem and inner strength. The initiative is founded on the belief that every girl possesses unique strengths, abilities and potential that can be nurtured and developed. Just as a small seed contains the potential to grow into a magnificent tree, every individual has the potential for growth and excellence. In the same way, every girl has a Tejaswini within her. Tejaswini Abhiyaan seeks to encourage girls to recognise their strengths, discover their potential and develop the confidence to make a positive contribution to their families, communities and society. Through educational, cultural, creative and community activities, the initiative promotes positive values, confidence, leadership, self-awareness, personal development and an appreciation of Indian cultural heritage, traditions and values. The initiative provides an encouraging environment where girls can learn, participate, express themselves, develop their abilities and build confidence while connecting with cultural traditions and the wider community. The vision of Tejaswini Abhiyaan is simple: to inspire every girl to discover, nurture and awaken the Tejaswini within her.

`technology` `free`

Talk to a Planner sessions help residents, business owners, community groups and industry professionals understand Brisbane City Plan 2014 (City Plan) and how it relates to their building and construction plans. Talk to a Planner is available all year round, with additional sessions held regularly across the city for your convenience. During these free sessions you can speak face-to-face with a planning specialist, giving you the opportunity to ask any questions you may have about how City Plan relates to your property. Council also has a team of dedicated officers who can answer simple questions about your development application over the phone during business hours (9am-4pm, Monday-Friday). To speak to an officer, phone Council on 07 3403 8888.

#### [Clock Tower Tour](https://www.eventbrite.com.au/e/clock-tower-tours-tickets-869613355957)
📆 Sat 26 Sep, 10:15 AM
📍 Museum of Brisbane, Brisbane City, Museum of Brisbane, Brisbane City Hall, 64 Adelaide Street, Brisbane City
💰 Free
`history` `tour` `free`

Discover the hidden secrets of your heritage-listed City Hall building with a guided tour of Brisbane’s iconic Clock Tower. The Clock Tower Tour is a “must do” while in Brisbane! For many years, City Hall’s Clock Tower elevated the building to Brisbane’s tallest, offering visitors a magnificent 360 degree view of the city around them. Whilst the view has changed significantly over the last 90 years, the time-honoured tradition of “taking a trip up the tower” happily continues at Museum of Brisbane. The Clock Tower Tour includes a ride in one of Brisbane’s oldest working cage lifts, a look behind Australia’s largest analogue clock faces and time to explore the observation platform that shares a unique perspective of your city. See if you can catch a glimpse of the bells! Make a booking online today to secure your spot! Spaces are limited and fill up quickly. Date & Time 10:15am - 4:45pm Daily, every 15 minutes Please arrive 15 minutes before your tour.

The Breakfast Creek Hotel is a popular hotspot for locals and tourists alike and after a short walk from the landing dock, it is where you can enjoy a delicious meal. Plating up a range of dishes such as Rump steak, Panko Calamari and Caesar Salad, it will be hard to leave the Hotel with a rumbling stomach. From here you will once again board the MV Neptune to cruise back to the dock at South Bank within the luscious Parklands.

💰 Free
`art` `design` `technology` `community` `free`

Step into a workshop buzzing with ideas and the potential to transform your artwork into merchandise. Drawing on the diverse practices of the AMPLIFY ME! Outdoor Gallery artists, this engaging session explores creative pathways from concept to product. Hear real-world examples, practical tips and personal stories from artists who are building sustainable creative careers through their work. Expect a lively, collaborative environment where connections are made, ideas are sparked and the seeds of your own creative future begin to take shape. This program is part of Council’s latest Outdoor Gallery exhibition, AMPLIFY ME! , a celebration of local Brisbane artists with lived experience of disability that showcases their work in accessible outdoor spaces across the city. Grounded in inclusive arts practice, the exhibition encourages collaboration and barrier-free creativity, supporting lasting connections, inclusion and social change across Brisbane’s cultural landscape. AMPLIFY ME! is curated by Nicole Crosswell and runs from 21 November 2025 to 25 October 2026. Council’s Outdoor Gallery transforms Brisbane’s laneways, streets and car parks into imaginative spaces for everyone, showcasing art in light boxes, banners, vitrines and evening projections. Image Credit: Ruby Herrenberg with Amplify Me! Amplify You!! 2025 Artist Collaboration located in Edward Street. Photography by Joseph Rucklie.

#### [Sukundimi Walks Before Me](https://www.fivestarcinemas.com.au/new-farm/movie/sukundimi-walks-before-me)
📆 Mon 28 Sep, 6:00 PM
📍 New Farm Cinemas, 701 Brunswick St, New Farm
💰 See link
`indigenous` `activism` `environment` `history`

Q&A Panel screening. New Farm Cinemas: Mon 28 September. Q&A with Manu Peni. The Sepik River is the mother line for Papua New Guinea communities. Winding through mountains and rainforests, she is the crucial vertebrae connecting and supporting the region’s rare biodiversity and spiritual consciousness. But her livelihood and her communities are threatened by the proposal of a copper-gold mine being built near her waters, which could extract, erode and pollute an environment that she has sustained for millennia. The children of this river, led by Manu Peni, create a grassroots campaign to stop the mine from being built, resisting the forces of colonial bureaucracy and Western narratives of ‘development’ by invoking the Spirit of the river and indigenous knowledge. Sukundimi Walks Before Me explores this existential fight through lyrical expressions of existence, resistance and life along the mother river.

#### [Power Nadeshiko Women's Empowerment Project community screening](https://www.eventbrite.com.au/e/power-nadeshiko-womens-empowerment-project-screening-session-brisbane-tickets-1994916040579)
📆 Sat 3 Oct, 9:30 AM
📍 Garden City Library, Garden City Library, Westfield Mt Gravatt, Level R4, corner of Logan Road and Kessels Road, Upper Mount Gravatt
💰 See website
`activism` `community` `women`

Join us for a free community screening and panel discussion to learn practical knowledge that could one day help you, your family, your friends, or someone in need. Together, we can build a stronger and more compassionate community. The Power Nadeshiko Women's Empowerment Project is a community-led initiative that empowers and educates culturally and linguistically diverse communities to address gender-based and domestic and family violence through multilingual educational resources and community engagement. Discussion topics include: mental health and domestic and family violence postnatal depression misogyny and gender-based discrimination technology-facilitated abuse coercive control laws children living with trauma and intergenerational trauma elder abuse survivor stories. Program: 9.30am - Welcome and opening 9.55–10.45am - Community screening 10.45–11.35am - Panel discussion 11.35–11.45am - Feedback and project updates 11.45am–12.15pm - Networking and morning tea (complimentary). Venue: Garden City Library, Meeting Room. Address: Level R4, Yellow Car Park, Westfield Mount Gravatt, Cnr Logan Road & Kessels Rd, Upper Mount Gravatt QLD 4122. Free parking available. The project is proudly funded by the Queensland Government through the Empowering Multicultural Community Projects Grant.

#### [Deinzel Morris - Book launch](https://www.trumba.com/calendars/brisbane-city-council?eventid=206670635)
📆 Sat 3 Oct, 11:00 AM
📍 Roma Street Parkland, Brisbane City, Roma Street Parkland, 1 Parkland Boulevard, Brisbane City
💰 Free
`literature` `writing` `books` `free`

Join local author Deinzel Morris on the Banyan Lawn as she launches her new book 'Holy Shigamoley, WTF Just Happened?' with the Brisbane public. What happens when your life falls apart so completely that the only way forward is through the wreckage? In Holy Shigamoley, WTF Just Happened? , Deinzel Morris opens the door to the wildest, darkest, funniest, and most transformational chapter of her life. From closing a teaching business and stepping into a dangerous new world, to drugs, love, heartbreak, psychosis, hospital wards, spiritual signs, family bonds, and the long road back to herself, this memoir is a brutally honest account of what it means to break, survive, heal, and keep laughing through the chaos. Told with raw humour, deep vulnerability, and no polished filter, this is not a story about having all the answers. It is a story about losing your mind, finding your truth, and learning that even in the messiest parts of life, love can still pull you home. Messy. Dark. Funny. Honest. Human. This is chaos, truth, love, and transformation - exactly as it happened.

This is a show that will leave you breathless. Visceral and high-octane, ESCAPE blends complex, moving architectural structures with multi-talented performers to create a blood-pumping and emotional multimedia experience. Along with DIAVOLO’s signature athletic movement aesthetic, ESCAPE features a collection of the company’s most famous architectural structures. Each evokes a story unique to its design, echoing themes of exploration, danger, fear, survival, resilience, and connection, all brought to life through a soundtrack spanning decades and genres, from pop and EDM to rock. DIAVOLO | Architecture in Motion® productions explore innovation and celebrate the complexity of the human condition with an undefinable blend of dance, stunt work, acrobatics, circus arts, and pedestrian movement. 💰 From $104
`music` `orchestra` `classical` `dance` `performance` `live music`

A celebration of music that moves the body and soul. From folk steps to final farewells, this concert is built around the joy of dance. At its heart is Rachmaninov’s final major work Symphonic Dances. Written in exile, it is filled with sweeping emotion, nostalgia, and the echoes of a life in motion. Led by Chief Conductor Umberto Clerici, it’s a powerful blend of elegance and vitality complete with an unforgettable saxophone solo. The evening begins with Dvořák’s Slavonic Dances, bursting with Bohemian spirit and irresistible folk rhythms. Then comes soloist James Crabb for the Queensland premiere of Holly Harrison’s Concerto for Classical Accordion and Orchestra, a bold, high-voltage work that dances to its own beat. Queensland Symphony Orchestra is proudly supported by Brisbane City Council.

#### [English linked sword dance](https://www.trumba.com/calendars/brisbane-city-council?eventid=196055085)
📆 Tue 29 Sep, 5:00 PM
📍 Moorooka Community Centre, Moorooka, Moorooka Community Centre, 40 Gainsborough Street, Moorooka
💰 See link
`music` `live music` `dance` `performance` `culture`

Step into the rhythm of steel and tradition with English Linked Sword Dance. This striking folk dance brings together precision, teamwork, and centuries of English heritage. Dancers move in tight formations, each holding a sword linked to the next, creating intricate patterns, locks, and woven figures that flow seamlessly with the music. The highlight comes when the swords are joined to form a star or “lock,” lifted high in a dramatic display of skill and unity. Energetic, powerful, and deeply communal, English linked sword dance is as much about cooperation as it is about spectacle. No solo stars here - every movement depends on trust, timing, and connection with the whole team. Whether you’re drawn by history, live music, or the thrill of learning something bold and different, this is your chance to be part of a living tradition. Join us, take a sword, and become one of the links.

The project channels a love of ’80s post punk, modern psych-pop, and atmospheric post-punk into a sound that feels expansive yet intimate. Formed against the backdrop of Brisbane’s tightly knit independent music community, Ancient Channels quickly stood out for their ability to fuse shimmering textures with grounded, emotionally honest lyricism. Their songs unfold like scenes with rich layers of reverb, swirling effects, and melodic guitar interplay creating a sonic world that invites listeners in rather than overwhelms.The result is music that feels both nostalgic and exploratory: echoes of The Cure and Siouxsie And The Banshees woven into something distinctly their own. Onstage, Ancient Channels delivers immersive performances marked by dynamic energy and detailed soundscapes. The band’s live sets move fluidly from atmospheric introspection to powerful crescendos, showcasing a versatility that resonates with audiences across Brisbane’s venues and festivals. With each release, Ancient Channels continues to refine and expand their sonic palette, building a reputation as one of Brisbane’s most compelling underground acts. Their work captures the restless creativity of the city’s scene while carving out a unique identity that is textured and heartfelt. [button link="https://events.humanitix.com/ancient-channels-static-spirit-album-launch"]Tickets[/button]

What is Vermilion High? Vermilion High is a program initiated by QUT’s student-run record label Vermilion Records. The program is the first of its kind in the country and offers a few talented high school musicians the chance to participate in a collaborative production process with final-year creative arts undergraduates from QUT. For final-year QUT students, at the brink of emerging into their own professional careers, they are able to refine their skills in the music business through production, marketing, artist liaising, and event management, to name just a few. For the Vermilion High students, these lucky young artists gain the opportunity to professionally record their original music at a state-of-the-art facility. It's a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity, and the Vermilion High program empowers these emerging artists to showcase their talent to the real world. The artists performing include: Evie May Braden Judge Sophie Nicholls Kira Green Liv Welsh Sabrina Rees Seb Tyne Prom In The Woods Glass Rain Ethan Dahl Asher Iyer Ruby Elizabeth [button link="https://pay.qut.edu.au/FCIEDUSJCONFERENECES/booking?UDS_ACTION=S1I&UDS_ACTION_DATA=FVxWcjdAXQROXVECNEJDBS1SQnRBXUQbVTJALARBUDEBRllS"] Tickets[/button] Vermilion Records - https://www.instagram.com/vermilion.records?utm_source=ig_web_button_share_sheet&igsi=ZDNlZDc0MzIxNw== Vermilion High - https://www.instagram.com/vermilion.high?utm_source=ig_web_button_share_sheet&igsi=ZDNlZDc0MzIxNw==

World-Class Voices. One Unforgettable Show. Following the success of Timeless Duets, a sold-out season of The Two Tenors – The Best of Josh Groban at Wynnum Fringe Festival, and a performance to more than 1,600 people at the prestigious Lord Mayor's City Hall Concerts, Vavachi Entertainment is delighted to return to Sandgate Town Hall with its exciting new production, The Two Tenors – The Best of Josh Groban. As part of Queensland Seniors Month, we would love to invite your members to enjoy this uplifting morning of world-class entertainment. Starring former The Ten Tenors member Roger Davy, Gold Palm Award-winning musical theatre performer Matthew Leigh, and special guest soprano Gabrielle Diaz from Opera Queensland and The 7 Sopranos, this inspiring concert celebrates the timeless music of Josh Groban alongside much-loved classical crossover favourites. Audiences will enjoy songs including You Raise Me Up, The Prayer, To Where You Are, Bridge Over Troubled Water, Time to Say Goodbye, and many more, together with a wonderful selection of Broadway favourites, light opera and contemporary classics. Together, these three exceptional artists deliver an elegant and uplifting concert experience filled with warmth, humour and breathtaking vocal brilliance. Three Voices. One Extraordinary Morning. Pure Vocal Brilliance.

📆 Thu 24 Sep – Fri 25 Sep
📍 Seven Hills Hub, Seven Hills Hub, 28 Tallowwood Street, Seven Hills
💰 See website
`theatre` `performance` `kids` `family`

Be transported to a soft and cosy woodland, right out of a picture book, where each new season is full of natural wonders waiting to be discovered by young, inquisitive minds. Enjoy an engaging and interactive story by the campfire before embarking on your own sensory exploration of the woods. Ages: Tailor made for audiences aged 0-3 and filled with performance, puppetry and play ‘What’s In The Woods?’ is a gentle welcome to the world of theatre. Sessions are split into 3 age groups running at various times: 0-1 years 1-2 years 2-3 years. Run time: Approximately 45 minutes, 20 minutes of performance and 25 minutes of play in the space. Acccessibility: Wheelchair accessible, baby changing facilities provided onsite and in space. Pram parking available. Carer resources available. Breakout space available. Reviews: “Fantastic. Such a safe and welcoming space.” “Incredibly skilled in their craft.” About the program: What's in the Woods? has been developed as part of the new Backbone Theatre for Babies program, developed with company-in-residence Brymore Productions. Backbone Theatre for Babies has been assisted by the Australian Government through Creative Australia, it’s arts funding and advisory body. Supported by: Backbone is supported by the Queensland Government through Arts Queensland. Backbone Youth Arts is proudly supported by Brisbane City Council.

#### [Lord Mayor's Seniors Cabaret Showcase](https://www.trumba.com/calendars/LIVE?eventid=205969592)
📆 Thu 24 Sep, 11:00 AM
📍 Arana Leagues Club, Keperra, Arana Leagues Club, 247 Dawson Parade, Keperra
💰 Free
`music` `singing` `dance` `performance` `seniors` `free`

The Lord Mayor's Seniors Cabaret is a free program for Brisbane seniors. It offers expert guidance and helps develop creative talents in a supportive, non-competitive environment. Prepare, deliver, succeed - if you can dream it, you can do it! This year's theme is Legends and icons: the people, moments and music that shaped our world. This theme is a powerful way to celebrate the extraordinary individuals, defining moments and unforgettable music that have shaped generations. After completing the masterclasses, some participants perform in a showcase which are held across the city. Through song, music, dance and spoken word, performers bring to life a soundtrack of social change, unity, hope and celebration. Come along and support these wonderful, talented seniors at a Lord Mayor's Cabaret Showcase. The Lord Mayor's Seniors Cabaret program is producer by Chris Fennessy, Epiphany Productions (Aust).

#### [LINKIN PARK: UNSHATTER](https://www.fivestarcinemas.com.au/new-farm/movie/linkin-park-unshatter)
📆 Wed 30 Sep, 6:45 PM
📍 New Farm Cinemas, 701 Brunswick St, New Farm
💰 See link
`music` `live music` `performance`

Premiere screening. New Farm Cinemas and Tower Cinemas Newcastle. The hardest part of ending is starting again. After a seven-year hiatus following the devastating loss of their beloved bandmate Chester Bennington, LINKIN PARK’S remaining members struggle to find their way forward. Their shared history and enduring friendship, combined with the fresh perspective of two new band members spark a renewed creative energy that leads them on a transformative journey of artistic reinvention. Following the band from intimate 2022 studio sessions through their explosive FROM ZERO album release and concert in São Paulo, the film UNSHATTER weaves together rare vault footage, electrifying performances from sold-out shows, and candid interviews with band members and fans—capturing the raw, honest story of what it means to start over.
